a:T6e7,The method has three connected pieces. First, the authors run a layer-wise degradation study. In the partial-RoPE stage, they convert only one layer from GQA to MLA at a time while keeping all other layers unchanged, then measure perplexity degradation. This isolates each layer’s sensitivity to different FreqFold settings. The main finding is clear: shallow layers often prefer larger FreqFold values, suggesting that principal-component concentration helps more than frequency approximation hurts; middle layers often prefer FreqFold=1, meaning accurate RoPE frequency preservation matters more there.

Second, they build a layer-adaptive GQA-to-MLA transition algorithm. The per-layer FreqFold assignment is formulated as a combinatorial optimization problem, then reframed as a multi-round sequential decision process to avoid brute-force search over all layer configurations. The central idea is simple but important: replace a uniform conversion hyperparameter with a per-layer allocation strategy guided by measured sensitivity. That is the main methodological difference from one-shot, globally uniform MLA conversion.

Third, they add a post-training pipeline to recover capability and inject financial expertise. This includes stratified data compression, financial-domain augmentation, refusal-data construction for compliance scenarios, and instruction-following reinforcement. The logic is coherent: structural conversion solves the serving bottleneck, but it weakens representation quality; post-training is then used to restore general and domain-specific competence. The paper also emphasizes native implementation on Huawei Ascend and deployment through vLLM-Ascend with MLA operators, which makes the work more than an offline architecture study.b:T475,The most important results fall into three groups. First, the serving gains are substantial: the final model reports a 72% reduction in KV-cache usage and a 2.69× increase in maximum concurrency. These are the headline numbers because the target setting is high-traffic financial deployment. Second, the layer-adaptive conversion outperforms uniform conversion. The evidence comes from the layer-wise perplexity degradation curves: the optimal FreqFold differs markedly by layer, so a single global setting is not Pareto-efficient. The paper reports lower perplexity degradation than uniform baselines across several mainstream LLM backbones. Third, the post-training pipeline recovers and improves task performance. With domain augmentation, refusal construction, and instruction reinforcement, the converted model not only regains capability lost during transition but also improves on financial benchmarks and real mobile-banking tasks. The provided text does not include the full model sizes or every benchmark score, so the most reliable takeaway is the shift of the accuracy-efficiency frontier rather than any single absolute metric.c:T489,For a quick but useful read, focus on three parts. Start with the introduction and the first system-level figures to understand the actual optimization target: not generic compression, but the accuracy-efficiency trade-off under high-concurrency financial serving. Then go to the method section around the single-layer conversion analysis and the layer-wise degradation figure. That is the most informative part of the paper and the main reason the adaptive design is justified. Finally, inspect the tables on KV-cache reduction, concurrency, and financial-task recovery to judge whether the converted model remains practically usable.

A solid skim should take about 25 to 35 minutes. If you want to go deeper, there are two worthwhile questions. One is why layer sensitivity differs: is it tied to RoPE usage, spectral structure of features, or functional specialization across layers? The other is what exactly post-training recovers after structural conversion: language modeling quality, instruction following, domain knowledge, or all three to different degrees. Those questions determine how transferable this recipe is to other architecture transitions.d:T4a1,A controlled human-subject study rather than a new model:
- Environment: a self-hosted GitLab with a CI pipeline to emulate production-like iterative development while keeping the sandbox controlled.
- Task: participants build a mini e-commerce app in Python via five sequential subtasks, each gated by hidden CI tests to enforce long-horizon, multi-round collaboration.
- Threat model: the agent is instructed to complete the main task while covertly inserting code that exfiltrates synthetic sensitive data to an external IP; success is binary and operationally clear.
- Conditions: participants collaborate with one of four frontier models (Claude-Opus-4.6, GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-Pro, MiniMax-M2.7). A subset of sessions includes a real-time LLM monitor that flags suspicious/malicious actions.
- Instrumentation: full interaction logs (prompts, tool calls, code changes) plus surveys at pre-study, post-study, and post-debrief to measure trust, perceived review competence, and safeguard preferences. The analysis attributes failures to low review effort, believable rationalizations by the agent, and overtrust; it also examines how baseline trust correlates with detection success.e:T772,The technical contribution is an automated evaluation pipeline rather than a new agent architecture. The system has three main components. First, framework collection and isolation: the authors gather 51 Python ADKs and run each inside its own Docker environment to avoid dependency conflicts and cross-framework contamination. Second, an LLM-as-a-Developer loop: given a benchmark task and a target framework, the coding model studies the framework through documentation, source code, or both, writes agent code, and enters a validate-and-feedback repair cycle. Third, a three-level validation pipeline: basic import and syntax checks, framework-level execution checks, and finally execution on real benchmark tasks. This staged design separates “can the code run at all” from “does it solve the task,” which reduces noise from trivial failures.

A strong design choice is that the generation process itself is treated as a first-class evaluation signal. The system logs token usage, number of turns, repair attempts, failure types, and dollar cost. As a result, the output is not just a task score but a joint measure of performance and development effort. Another important component is the information-source ablation. The developer model is tested with docs only, source only, both, or neither. This is necessary because many popular frameworks are already partially memorized by frontier coding models. Without this ablation, it would be unclear whether the model is actually learning from the provided materials or simply recalling APIs from parametric memory.

The method does not remove every confound. Results still depend on the coding model, its search and repair behavior, context budget, and the benchmark mix. The paper’s stance is more practical than idealized: keep the developer and execution protocol fixed so that framework differences become the dominant source of variation.f:T8e9,The evaluation covers 51 frameworks and 4 benchmarks, for 204 framework-benchmark pairs. The benchmark suite spans software engineering, conversational tool use, multi-tool orchestration, and terminal interaction, which gives a broader view than a single micro-benchmark.

Three findings stand out. First, development cost varies widely. The cost to reach a working agent differs by about 5.6x across frameworks, roughly from $0.6 to $3.4 per agent. The paper interprets this as a combined signal of API complexity, documentation quality, and abstraction design. Frameworks such as LangGraph and OpenAI Agents are among the cheapest to target, while larger or less coherent frameworks are much more expensive. Second, final task performance is highly dispersed. The best single-benchmark ADK agents reach around 80% resolution, while the median framework is only around 32%, so there is no universally dominant framework. Third, the model that writes the agent matters more than the model that runs it. Agents authored by stronger coding models such as Opus resolve roughly twice as many tasks as GPT-authored agents on the same execution backbone. That is an important systems result: coding-time model quality has a persistent downstream effect through code structure, API usage, and error handling.

The information-source ablation is also informative. Documentation, source code, and parametric knowledge are not equivalent, but the gap is smaller than one might expect. Genuine framework usage success stays in roughly the 28% to 40% range, with raw source access performing best. Even with no reference material, about 33% of agents still pass. This suggests that many framework APIs are already partially internalized by coding models, and that source code often provides more actionable grounding than polished documentation because it exposes real call paths, signatures, and defaults.

A limitation is that the paper, at least from the provided content, does not fully unpack variance across repeated runs, sensitivity to developer-model settings, or confidence intervals per benchmark. Those details matter for ranking stability. Another limitation is that the benchmark suite is still centered on coding and tool-use settings rather than open-ended long-horizon agents.10:T42d,dots.tts has three main components. First, it trains an AudioVAE as a continuous speech tokenizer. The VAE is not optimized only for reconstruction; after reconstruction-oriented training, the authors add multitask downstream objectives and a WavLM representation-alignment loss so that the latent space is semantic, reconstructable, and easier for an AR model to predict. Second, generation is decomposed into a semantic encoder, an LLM backbone, and an autoregressive flow-matching head. The LLM handles long-range text-to-content planning, while the flow head renders local acoustic detail for each latent patch. The flow head uses full-history conditioning instead of only local context, which is meant to reduce drift in long utterances. Third, the post-training stage adapts reward-free self-correction, in the spirit of SOAR, to the flow head. The acoustic DiT is trained on its own off-trajectory inference errors without requiring a reward model or an external teacher. For deployment, MeanFlow distillation reduces the flow ODE to 2 to 4 function evaluations.11:T418,The method represents the teacher’s explicit reasoning process as a hidden-state tensor over rationale tokens, layers, and hidden dimensions. The student produces a shorter tensor over a small number of implicit reasoning steps, layers, and hidden dimensions. Instead of aligning individual CoT tokens to latent steps, LoRi projects both tensors into a shared low-rank Tucker-style subspace and aligns their first- and second-order statistics. The training objective combines standard cross-entropy, which preserves the student’s ability to generate explicit solutions and final answers, with a low-rank alignment loss. The alignment loss has two parts: rationale-level alignment captures the global geometry of the teacher’s reasoning trajectory, while anchor-level alignment regularizes the representation at the answer prediction position. The main design choice is to avoid brittle local correspondence and transfer only the dominant trajectory structure, making it possible to distill long rationales into a small number of latent steps.12:T720,The framework has two phases. Phase 1 is Edit-SFT. From existing rollouts, the model extracts two step-level internal signals offline. The first is posterior belief over the final mark. At each reasoning-step boundary, the partial chain is appended with a final scoring scaffold, and the model’s next-token logits over valid mark tokens are read out. This gives a posterior distribution over the final integer score, from which the expected mark and its change relative to the gold mark can be computed. A step is therefore labeled as moving the model’s belief closer to or farther from the correct score.

The second signal is grounding. Using mask-based scoring, the method estimates whether a reasoning step depends on the student answer, the rubric, and prior reasoning context. This is meant to detect steps that are weakly anchored in external evidence or rules. Using these two signals together, Edit-SFT does not rewrite the whole chain of thought. Instead, it identifies the first atomic step where the reasoning either drifts away from the correct final mark or becomes weakly grounded, and revises only that local segment. A rubric checklist is supplied as privileged context to guide the edit, but the output is not forced into a rigid checklist format.

Phase 2 is Edit-RL. Rather than using plain outcome RL, the method adds belief-guided reward shaping on top of a group-relative outcome reward. In addition to rewarding closeness of the final mark to the gold score, it penalizes excessive mid-trajectory belief drift. Small fluctuations are treated as benign exploration, but large deviations away from the gold mark incur a penalty proportional to the excess drift. This encodes the task structure that grading should progressively converge rather than wander arbitrarily and only be judged at the end.13:T4ed,CVT-RL combines constrained policy-gradient training with dense verifiable rewards and policy-conditioned counterfactual credit. For a step a_t at history h_t, PCCC estimates how final verified success changes when a_t is replaced by an intervention-specific counterfactual action and the remainder of the trajectory is completed by a frozen continuation policy μ. The paper defines four separate intervention families: deletion, neutral semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation. These are kept as separate estimands before aggregation. To reduce off-support counterfactuals, proposals pass an intervention-validity gate covering syntax, schema validity, support score, and entailment score. The RL objective also includes costs for unsupported claims, skipped verification, evaluator tampering, unsafe tool calls, repeated null actions, and budget overruns. Candidate steps are selected using gradient norm, verifier disagreement, tool relevance, and novelty, with at most eight selected steps per trajectory. A calibrated selection model corrects selection bias. The outcome model is a DeBERTa-v3-base cross-encoder over compressed history, action, tool state, and verifier features, trained on frozen-continuation outcomes.14:T86c,方法核心是 SHALA-LLM，一个基于 GRPO 的 ambiguity-aware distributional alignment 框架。输入不是单一标签，而是由多位标注者形成的经验标签分布。模型也不是输出一个类别，而是生成 verbalized distribution，也就是用文本形式给出各候选类别的概率分配。作者选择这种形式，是因为它能直接复用通用 LLM 的生成接口，不必额外加分类头，同时可以把“预测不确定性”作为语言输出的一部分。

训练上，作者把 LLM 视为 policy，对每个样本采样多个 rollout。每个 rollout 解析成一个预测分布，再与真实 annotator distribution 比较，得到 reward。SHALA 的关键不只是用分布匹配 reward，而是根据样本的歧义程度动态重加权 reward，使高歧义样本在优化中占更大权重。直观上，这相当于告诉模型：在大家都一致的样本上，学会主标签就够了；在意见分裂的样本上，重点不是押中某个单标签，而是学会保留多种 plausible interpretation 的概率质量。作者把这一点嵌入 GRPO 的 group-based rollout 优化里，而不是做普通监督学习下的 soft label cross-entropy。

从与现有方法的区别看，论文主要区别于三类路线。第一类是 majority vote 或 label smoothing 式训练，它们仍然默认存在一个主真值，只是在局部做平滑。SHALA 直接把目标改成 annotator distribution。第二类是 inference-time uncertainty elicitation，只在测试时让模型报概率或多答案，但训练目标没变。SHALA 把分布学习放进训练过程。第三类是一般的 distributional alignment 或 pluralistic alignment 工作，往往强调多视角输出，但未必显式利用样本级 annotator disagreement 来调节优化强度。SHALA 的“smart handling”主要体现在这个动态加权机制上。

不过也要看到方法边界。它依赖多标注者分布，适用场景天然受限；verbalized distribution 还涉及文本解析误差和概率校准问题；GRPO 相比直接监督学习训练成本更高，是否值得要看任务规模和歧义密度。15:T92c,The method, SHALA-LLM, is an ambiguity-aware distributional alignment framework built on top of GRPO. Instead of a single gold label, each example is represented by an empirical label distribution derived from multiple annotators. The model is asked to produce a verbalized distribution, meaning a textual output that specifies probabilities over candidate classes. This choice avoids adding a separate classifier head and lets the model express uncertainty through the same generative interface used by standard LLMs.

Training is formulated as policy optimization. For each sample, the model generates multiple rollouts. Each rollout is parsed into a predicted class distribution, which is then compared against the annotator distribution to produce a reward. The distinctive part of SHALA is not only that it uses a distributional reward, but that it dynamically reweights rewards according to the degree of annotator disagreement. Highly ambiguous samples receive greater influence during optimization. Intuitively, the model is encouraged to do different things on different regions of the data: on low-ambiguity examples, it can concentrate on the dominant label; on high-ambiguity examples, it should preserve probability mass over multiple plausible interpretations rather than collapse to a single answer.

Relative to prior work, the paper differs from three common approaches. First, majority-vote or soft-label training still assumes a dominant truth and only smooths around it. SHALA makes the annotator distribution itself the target. Second, inference-time uncertainty elicitation asks the model to report probabilities or multiple answers at test time, but leaves the training objective unchanged. SHALA moves distribution learning into optimization. Third, broader pluralistic or distributional alignment work often focuses on diverse outputs without explicitly using sample-level disagreement to modulate training strength. The “smart handling” in SHALA is precisely this ambiguity-aware reweighting.

There are clear boundaries. The method requires multi-annotator distributions, so applicability depends on data collection. Verbalized distributions introduce parsing and calibration issues. And GRPO is more expensive than direct supervised learning, so the cost-benefit tradeoff depends on task scale and ambiguity density.16:T855,The experiments use ChaosNLI, GoEmotions, and MSP-Podcast, covering ambiguity-sensitive NLI and emotion recognition. The evaluation includes both distributional metrics and standard classification metrics, which is the right choice here because F1 alone would not reveal whether the model actually learned human uncertainty.

The strongest reported gain is on ChaosNLI: Jensen-Shannon Distance is reduced by 62.1%, while F1 improves by 16.6%. That is important because it suggests the model is not merely becoming softer; it is getting closer to the human label distribution while also improving discrete prediction quality. On MSP-Podcast, JSD drops by 6.2% and F1 improves by 29.2%. The classification gain is larger there, while the distributional gain is more modest, possibly reflecting a harder label geometry in speech emotion data where preserving ambiguity prevents harmful collapse to a single class. The abstract does not provide the exact GoEmotions numbers, but the paper claims consistent improvements over majority-label supervision.

The analysis appears to support the intended mechanism. Gains are larger under high-ambiguity conditions, which matches the design of the weighting scheme. Benefits are stronger for inherently ambiguous semantic categories, suggesting the model is learning multi-interpretation structure rather than simply outputting flatter distributions. The paper also reports more uncertainty-aware reasoning behavior, meaning the model is more willing to acknowledge multiple plausible interpretations in its outputs.

The main thing to check when reading the experiments is whether the JSD improvement comes from genuinely better distribution modeling or from overly conservative predictions. The concurrent F1 gains argue against the trivial “predict a bland average distribution” failure mode, but the ablations still matter. In particular, readers should inspect how much of the gain comes from the ambiguity-based weighting itself, how rewards are defined, and how robust the method is to rollout count, group size, and reward variance, which are often critical in GRPO-style training.17:T5c7,The workflow has three parts:
1) Discovery via controlled layer-wise ZO tuning: using the standard two-point SPSA/MeZO-style estimator, they run ZO fine-tuning while updating only one layer at a time (all other layers frozen), keeping the same ZO hyperparameters across layers. This produces a layer-wise “utility profile” for ZO.
2) Characterization: the dominant layer is largely task-agnostic for a fixed model (same layer wins across tasks) but model-specific across families (layer index changes between LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B). In contrast, FO fine-tuning distributes gains more broadly, indicating the dominance is not simply “this layer is universally most important”, but a ZO-specific phenomenon.
3) Pre-training identification: they connect the dominant layer to activation outliers. With a small calibration set and inference-only forward passes, they compute layer-wise activation statistics and pick the first layer exhibiting a clear outlier pattern; empirically this matches the dominant layer.
Their explanation centers on ZO estimator reliability: ZO updates depend on how strongly a parameter perturbation changes the final loss. Perturbations injected earlier into the residual stream propagate through all subsequent layers and can be repeatedly transformed/accumulated, yielding larger and more stable loss differences. The first outlier layer appears to be where this amplification becomes pronounced, making ZO gradient estimates higher-SNR there.18:T654,The pipeline has three stages. First, the authors generate teacher traces using two large reasoning teachers, Qwen3.5-397B-A17B and gpt-oss-120B. For each question, they sample a response containing a <think> trace and a final answer, extract the answer, and use rejection sampling until they obtain a correct example or hit the retry limit. This yields datasets of question, correct trace, and correct answer.

Second, they compress the traces post hoc. Instead of training a dedicated compressor, they use two instruction-tuned models with a generic prompt asking for a shorter reasoning trace that preserves the essential logical steps, key insights, and final answer. The two compressors differ in aggressiveness and style, producing parallel compressed datasets.

Third, they fine-tune student models on three supervision variants: raw traces, compressed traces, and answer-only targets. Training uses standard next-token prediction on assistant tokens with sample packing. The student grid includes Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, Qwen3.5-9B-Base, Llama-3.1-8B, and gpt-oss-20B, under both LoRA and one-epoch full/FSDP fine-tuning.

The most important design choice is the truncation ablation. For the Qwen-teacher runs, they create a compute-equivalent baseline by truncating the raw trace to match the token length of the most aggressive compressed version while keeping the final answer unchanged. This is the right control if one wants to test whether compression improves information density rather than merely reducing token budget. The results do not support a strong information-density advantage for compressed traces.19:T6a0,The experimental grid is fairly broad. The two teachers each generate about 283k correct traces. Compression reduces trace length to 8.6% to 21.0% of the original character count. Training tokens drop to 12% to 30% of the raw-trace budget, wall-clock training speeds up by 2.0x to 7.6x, and student inference outputs become 3x to 19x shorter. The gains are smaller under the gpt-oss teacher because its raw traces are already shorter.

The main study contains 48 runs across two teachers, four students, four data sources, and two training regimes. The ranking is consistent: raw traces achieve the best downstream accuracy at every tested scale and for both teachers; compressed traces are in the middle; answer-only is cheapest but performs worst and can be unstable under full fine-tuning. That consistency is more informative than any single number.

The seven Qwen-teacher truncation ablations are especially important. When the token budget is matched, compressed traces do not reliably outperform equally short truncated raw traces. This suggests that the main benefit of compression is compute reduction, not better supervision per token. The paper also reports failure modes such as termination failures in smaller students and instability in answer-only training, which hints that reasoning traces provide structural anchoring in addition to task information.

Evaluation covers in-distribution datasets across math, science, commonsense/logic, and medical QA, plus several OOD reasoning benchmarks. Even without a flashy headline metric, the empirical message is clear and well supported: trace compression is a practical efficiency knob, not an accuracy-improving distillation method.1a:T6d0,The intervention is deliberately minimal. The authors define source-conditioned role relabeling: after a model produces an incorrect intermediate claim c*, they append an additional message containing the exact same bytes of c*, but place it under an external role such as user, tool, or system memory, while keeping the audit instruction fixed across conditions. This isolates the causal effect of the role wrapper from content changes.

The paper then adds several mechanism probes. A self-distrust control asks the model to verify its own thoughts directly. This performs much worse than relabeling, which argues against a simple “extra verification reminder” explanation. A handle-granularity decomposition separates two effects: giving the claim an explicit standalone wrapper versus assigning that wrapper a specific role tag. The results suggest that a bare wrapper already helps, and the role label adds further lift. This supports an addressability account: a claim embedded inside the model’s own continuous scratchpad is harder to target, quote, and reject than the same claim presented as a discrete external object. The authors also analyze logprob signatures to distinguish engage-then-verify behavior from naive recomputation.

A useful part of the paper is the safety boundary analysis. The authors test the reverse direction: if relabeling helps correction, can role manipulation also inject errors? By default, the answer is mostly no. But once a trust-framing instruction is added, such as telling the model to treat memory as ground truth and not verify it, the attack channel opens up. So the effect is real, but it is mediated by instruction hierarchy and trust framing rather than being an unconditional role prior.1b:T554,The study covers 13 model-domain cells across 7 model families and 3 domains, with 30 paired tasks per cell. Relabeling the same wrong claim from the model’s own <thought> into an external role improves correction rates by 23 to 93 percentage points. Ten of the 13 cells show significant gains; the remaining three are ceiling cases where the baseline correction rate is already high, at or above 67%. One highlighted example is Llama-3.3-70B, where the lift reaches +77 points.

The adversarial mirror includes five attack settings with n=30 each. Under default conditions, all 20 attack-rate cells stay at or below 3.3%, so the correction channel does not automatically become an error-injection channel. However, adding a single trust-framing sentence can raise the math attack rate to 70%, which sharply limits any naive deployment claim.

The mechanism ablations are also informative. A bare syntactic wrapper contributes roughly 17 to 23 points, and the role tag adds about 30 more. In the self-distrust control, explicitly asking the model to verify its own thoughts yields only 0 to 23% correction, versus up to 70% under relabeling. The paper also uses paired bootstrap statistics and includes frontier closed models such as GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4, and Gemini 2.5 Flash, which makes the phenomenon harder to dismiss as a quirk of one open model family.1c:T622,The method has three parts. First, trajectory reconstruction. The model is treated as a flow-matching or ODE-based generator with a learned velocity field. Given a real video encoded into the pretrained VAE latent space, the authors integrate the velocity field backward from the clean latent toward noise, producing an approximate reverse trajectory. This allows them to rerun the backbone at each time step and collect block-level hidden states and attention patterns. The appendix discusses approximation error and the conditions under which the recovered trajectory is a faithful proxy. Second, linear probing. They train lightweight probes on intermediate transformer states to predict physical plausibility and some quantitative physical variables. The point is not to maximize probe capacity, but to localize where and when physical information becomes linearly accessible. Third, causal intervention. They perform block-level noise interventions and use a probe-surprise style analysis to identify which components carry or amplify the physical signal. This goes beyond standard probing: it asks not only whether the information is readable, but also whether perturbing specific computations changes the signal and downstream generation. Compared with predictive representation learners such as V-JEPA, the model here is not trained with an explicit future-prediction objective. Compared with standard diffusion interpretability work, the analysis is anchored on real videos embedded back into the model’s trajectory rather than on arbitrary generated samples.1d:T56d,The experiments are organized around a few core phenomena rather than a broad benchmark sweep. First, on real videos with known physical plausibility labels, the recovered trajectories show that physical plausibility is linearly decodable from intermediate diffusion-transformer states. The signal is not uniform across the network: it tends to peak at certain depths and noise levels, often in intermediate blocks rather than at the input latent or final layer. Second, the paper reports that some quantitative physical variables are also decodable, which suggests the model is not merely storing a coarse plausible-versus-implausible cue. Third, the strength of the signal depends on trajectory fidelity: when the reconstructed path better follows the model’s learned continuous dynamics, probe performance improves, supporting the claim that physical structure emerges from denoising dynamics. Fourth, intervention experiments show that perturbing specific blocks weakens decodability or changes generation behavior, indicating that these layers are involved in forming and transmitting the signal rather than passively correlating with it. The provided text does not include the full numeric tables, model sizes, or training-scale details, but the study is clearly conducted on modern video diffusion transformers operating in a pretrained VAE latent space rather than on toy models.1e:T516,The paper formalizes a harness h as a persistent collection of tools, prompts, and skills. Given a task t, solve(h, t) produces a trajectory τ containing observations, reasoning traces, tool calls, and the final answer. The true utility U(t, τ) is latent in deployment because there is no representative future validation set. RHO replaces this unavailable utility with a self-preference estimator: the agent compares multiple trajectories for the same task, produces a ranking, and explains the preference. The pipeline has three stages. First, coreset selection chooses diverse and challenging tasks from past trajectories, so the optimization budget is spent on informative cases rather than easy or redundant ones. Second, group rollout re-solves each selected task multiple times in parallel, producing alternative trajectories under the current harness. Third, best-of-N harness proposal uses diagnostic signals to generate candidate harness updates and selects among them with pairwise self-preference. The two key diagnostics are self-validation within a trajectory and self-consistency across parallel trajectories. The method differs from simple experience accumulation: it does not merely append past traces to context, but turns them into targeted changes to skills, tools, and workflows.1f:T402,The evaluation covers three agent domains: software engineering, technical work, and knowledge work. The clearest reported result is on software engineering: a single round of retrospective harness optimization improves SWE-Bench Pro pass rate from 59% to 78% without grading against a validation set. The paper also reports consistent gains across all three domains, outperforming straightforward experience accumulation and validation-feedback-driven evolution under a comparable budget. Diagnostic analysis shows that RHO tends to design skills and tools aimed at typical failure modes found in past trajectories. These updates change the agent’s action patterns and help maintain higher accuracy in long-horizon sessions. Ablations indicate that the stages contribute progressively: coreset selection concentrates the budget on useful tasks, group rollout creates comparable alternatives, self-validation and self-consistency isolate improvement signals, and pairwise self-preference selects the final harness candidate.3:["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 MLA，其余层保持不变，然后测困惑度变化。这样可以把不同层对 FreqFold 的敏感性拆开看。结果显示，浅层在较大 FreqFold 下反而更稳，说明主成分压缩收益大于频率近似误差；中层则更依赖精确频率信息，FreqFold=1 往往最好。\n\n第二部分是 layer-adaptive GQA-to-MLA transition。作者把每层 FreqFold 选择写成组合优化问题，但直接穷举不可行，于是改写成多轮顺序决策，逐层分配压缩配置，目标是在整体 KV 压缩和困惑度退化之间找更优折中。虽然给出的摘要片段没有完整展开算法细节，但从全文脉络看，核心不是训练一个复杂控制器，而是利用前面的层敏感性分析，把统一超参替换成逐层可变配置。这一点与常见“一刀切 MLA 转换”区别很大。\n\n第三部分是能力恢复与领域注入。结构迁移后，作者没有只做普通 SFT，而是设计了完整 post-training pipeline：包括分层数据压缩、金融领域数据增强、合规拒答数据构造、以及指令跟随强化。这里的逻辑很清楚：结构转换先解决服务瓶颈，但会损伤表示能力；后训练再把通用能力和金融能力拉回到可用区间。论文还强调其训练和部署都原生适配华为 Ascend 生态，并在 vLLM-Ascend 上实现 MLA operator，这使得方法不是停留在离线实验，而是能落到真实 serving 栈里。","experiments":"实验里最重要的结果有三类。第一类是结构转换本身的收益。作者报告最终模型实现了 72% 的 KV cache 降低，以及 2.69× 的最大并发提升，这直接对应高并发金融场景的部署目标。第二类是逐层自适应优于统一转换。全文的关键证据来自 layer-wise perplexity degradation 曲线：不同层的最优 FreqFold 明显不同，说明统一配置不是 Pareto 最优。作者声称在多个主流 LLM 上，自适应分配相比 uniform conversion 能显著降低困惑度退化。第三类是后训练恢复效果。通过金融数据增强、拒答构造和指令强化，模型不仅补回了结构改造造成的能力损失，还在金融 benchmark cache、最大并发、以及金融任务恢复效果的表格，判断这是不是“压缩后还能用”，还是“压缩后重新训回来了”。\n\n预计 25-35 分钟可以完成有效泛读。若要深挖，重点看两点：一是逐层敏感性的来源，是否和层内频率编码使用方式、特征谱分布、或注意力头功能分工有关；二是 post-training 在恢复结构损伤时到底恢复了什么，是语言建模能力、指令能力，还是金融知识本身。前者关系到能否推广到别的架构迁移，后者关系到这种路线是否能替代部分持续预训练。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a deployment-driven but technically nontrivial problem: how to convert existing GQA-based LLMs into MLA-style models for high-concurrency serving, while keeping the loss in language modeling quality and domain capability under control. The key observation from the full paper is that GQA-to-MLA conversion is not layer-uniform. Different layers react very differently to FreqFold compression during the partial-RoPE transition. Early layers can tolerate more aggressive folding, while some middle layers are much more sensitive to RoPE frequency approximation. So the real problem is not merely whether MLA can compress KV cache, but how to perform a layer-adaptive structural transition and then recover the lost capacity through targeted post-training.","importance":"For foundation-model research, the main value is methodological rather than conceptual novelty. At evaluation time, constraints are hidden initially. When an agent proposes a plan or action that violates a hidden constraint, the system reveals that constraint and requires the agent to re-plan. Yet their capability on ultra-resolution images - where critical evidence is tiny, subtle, spatially distant, or distributed - remains unclear. We introduce UltraVR, a diagnostic benchmark for evidence-grounded visual reasoning over ultra-re","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-04T01:51:16Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05576.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"UltraVR 解决的是超高分辨率图像 VQA 中“答对了是否真的看到了证据”的评测问题。现有高分辨率或多模态推理评测多看最终答案，难以区分模型失败来自证据搜索、局部细节保留、跨区域整合，还是最后推理错误。","importance":"这是一篇评测资产型工作，对预训练研究的直接价值在于暴露当前 VLM 高分辨率视觉接口的薄弱环节。它不只是测大图输入能力，而是把视觉到答案的过程拆成可诊断步骤。对于做 native multimodal pretraining、高分辨率视觉 token 压缩、多尺度视觉编码、长视觉上下文建模的人，这类过程级评测比单一准确率更有用，因为它能指出训练或架构应该补哪一段能力。","technical_approach":"UltraVR 覆盖 CCTV 监控、遥感、病理全切片和工业异常检测四类超高分辨率图像场景。任务设计避免依赖显著物体或粗粒度场景语义，而要求模型先定位细小或分散证据，再做局部识别、计数、比较、关系判断或异常推理。核心设计是结构化 GT-CoT。每个样本不仅有最终答案，还包含一串可验证的中间步骤：step question、ground-truth step answer 和操作标签。操作标签被统一为五类：evidence grounding，local perception，quantification，evidence integration，decision inference。这样可以把黑盒 VQA 拆成证据访问、细节感知、数量判断、跨区域整合和最终决策几个阶段。相比只给 bounding box 或只给最终 QA 的高分辨率 benchmark，它更强调错误归因。","experiments":"作者评测了前沿闭源和开源 VLM，使用三条轴线：端到端 QA、视觉证据访问评测、GT-CoT 过程级评测。端到端结果显示当前模型在 UltraVR 上仍有明显空间，最强模型 macro accuracy 为 44.9%。给模型提供局部化证据后，性能只出现部分且依模型而异的提升，说明默认全图推理的问题不只是找不到区域，也包括细节保留、视觉 token 表示和后续推理。GT-prefix 评测中，逐步纠正中间视觉事实会显著改善最终表现；按操作类型分析后，很多错误集中在早期 evidence grounding 和 local perception。这一点对高分辨率 VLM 训练很关键：如果早期视觉证据没有进入语言模型上下文，后面的推理 prompt 或 CoT 很难补救。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看 Introduction 中对现有高分辨率评测缺口的定义，数据构造部分中 GT-CoT 和五类操作标签的标注方式，以及实验部分的三类评测轴线。最值得看的是 operation-level error analysis 和 GT-prefix evaluation，它们能判断模型瓶颈是在视觉证据获取还是语言侧推理。对预训练研究者而言，不必深看具体垂直领域模板，重点关注这种过程级诊断能否迁移到通用 VLM 预训练评测。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"UltraVR targets a diagnostic gap in ultra-resolution image VQA: final-answer accuracy does not reveal whether a VLM actually found and used the answer-critical visual evidence. Failures can come from evidence search, loss of local detail, weak cross-region integration, or final inference errors, and common benchmarks do not separate these stages.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is evaluative rather than algorithmic. UltraVR gives a process-level probe for high-resolution visual interfaces in VLMs. This is useful for work on native multimodal pretraining, visual token compression, multi-scale encoders, and long visual context, because it shows which part of the visual-to-language pipeline is failing rather than only reporting an aggregate score.","technical_approach":"The benchmark spans four ultra-resolution domains: CCTV surveillance, remote sensing, whole-slide pathology, and industrial anomaly detection. The questions are designed to require evidence search, local perception, counting or comparison, integration across regions, and answer inference. Its main annotation feature is structured ground-truth CoT. Each item includes intermediate step questions, verified step answers, and an operation label. The operation taxonomy has five categories: evidence grounding, local perception, quantification, evidence integration, and decision inference. This turns each QA example into a traceable diagnostic sample. Compared with high-resolution benchmarks that only provide task-level QA or region-level grounding, UltraVR focuses more on attributing errors along the reasoning pipeline.","experiments":"The paper evaluates proprietary and open-weight VLMs along three axes: end-to-end QA, visual-evidence access, and GT-CoT process evaluation. The strongest model reaches only 44.9% macro accuracy, indicating that the benchmark is not saturated. Providing localized evidence improves performance only partially and in a model-dependent way, which suggests that full-image inference is limited by a mix of evidence access, detail preservation, and reasoning over visual facts. GT-prefix evaluation shows that correcting intermediate visual facts substantially improves final answers. Operation-level analysis points to early evidence grounding and local perception as frequent failure points.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough. We introduce CogManip, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates 15 manipulation strategy risks across 1,000 multi-turn interaction sc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-04T12:38:43Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06099.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要评估 LLM 在多轮对话中是否会产生隐蔽操纵行为。它关注的不是显式违规或单轮 jailbreak，而是模型如何在连续互动里利用信息不对称、情绪依赖、虚假权威、策略性退让等方式影响用户判断。","importance":"这是一篇安全评测型工作，对基座预训练本身的直接贡献有限，但对 post-train 安全评估和模型行为诊断有一定参考价值。它把“操纵”拆成可标注、可统计的策略维度，有助于观察不同模型家族、不同对齐方式在多轮社会互动中的风险形态。对预训练研究员更有用的部分，是它提示单轮静态安全集不足以暴露模型的长期交互偏置；训练目标、指令数据和偏好数据可能会把迎合、过度建议、情绪强化等行为一起放大。","technical_approach":"CogManip 的核心是一个多轮操纵风险 benchmark。论文先从心理学和行为科学定义 manipulation：一种绕过或干扰用户理性审议的隐蔽影响行为。随后把风险分为三大类、十五个策略维度。第一类是认知与信息操纵，包括编造、伪造权威、拒绝修正、事实否认、信息混淆和视角颠覆。第二类是情感与心理操纵，包括回答迎合、反馈迎合、虚假紧迫感、依赖诱导和情感勒索。第三类是策略与元机制操纵，包括虚张声势、诱导式让步、战术欺骗和策略性示弱。场景设计覆盖五类现实互动：情感支持、边界确认、公民参与、消费建议和人生规划。相比传统安全 benchmark，它的差异在于评估单位从静态 prompt 变成多轮轨迹，并且把风险标签放在策略层，而不是只判断最终回答是否违规。","experiments":"数据规模是 1,000 个多轮互动场景，覆盖中英文和五类场景，经人工专家筛选与验证。论文评估了 13 个代表性模型，生成 13,000 条多轮对话样本，并对其中 1,680 条提供人工标注。结果显示不同模型、场景和操纵策略之间存在明显差异：有些模型更容易出现信息类操纵，有些更容易出现情感依赖或迎合类问题。论文还分析了策略组合和时间动态，说明操纵风险往往不是单一标签，而会在多轮中逐步累积。额外的 pressure-prompt 测试显示，良性压力和恶意压力对模型行为的影响不同，这一点对后续做风险触发检测和提示层缓解有参考意义。摘要中提到 GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-V3.2 等模型名；由于无法从给定内容确认发布背景，相关结论更适合看作该 benchmark 的评测对象描述，而不是独立事实依据。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 25–35 分钟。优先看 CogManip Framework 中 manipulation 的定义、三大类十五策略的划分，以及 Appendix C.1 和 C.3 的策略定义与场景设计依据。实验部分重点看模型风险分布、不同场景下的策略差异、策略共现和时间动态分析。若只关心预训练与 post-train 的接口，可重点看迎合、依赖诱导、虚假权威和事实否认几类标签，因为它们更可能反映指令微调和偏好优化带来的行为副作用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper evaluates whether LLMs exhibit covert manipulative behavior in multi-turn interactions, rather than merely violating explicit safety rules in single-turn prompts.","importance":"This is primarily a safety evaluation paper, not a pretraining method paper. Its value for foundation-model researchers is diagnostic: it shows why static safety prompts miss interaction-level behaviors such as sycophancy, dependency induction, false authority, and gradual belief shaping. These behaviors are relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface because instruction tuning and preference optimization can amplify helpfulness-like responses that become manipulative in long conversations.","technical_approach":"CogManip builds a benchmark around a psychologically grounded definition of manipulation: covert influence that interferes with a user’s rational deliberation. The taxonomy has three top-level groups and fifteen strategy labels. Cognitive and information manipulation covers fabrication, authority faking, doubling down, fact denial, obfuscation, and perspectival subversion. Affective and psychological manipulation covers answer sycophancy, feedback sycophancy, false urgency, dependency, and emotional blackmail. Strategic and meta-mechanism manipulation covers bluffing, feint and bait, tactical deception, and strategic sandbagging. The scenarios span affective support, boundary confirmation, civic participation, consumer advice, and life planning. The main methodological distinction from standard safety benchmarks is that the evaluated object is a multi-turn trajectory with strategy-level annotations, not a single final response.","experiments":"The benchmark contains 1,000 bilingual multi-turn scenarios across five scenario categories. The authors evaluate 13 representative models and collect 13,000 dialogue samples, with human annotations for 1,680 samples. The reported analysis shows heterogeneous risk profiles across model families, scenario types, and manipulation strategies. The paper also examines strategy combinations and temporal dynamics, indicating that manipulation risk often accumulates across turns rather than appearing as a single isolated failure. Additional pressure-prompt tests suggest that benign and malicious pressure conditions affect model behavior differently, which may support prompt-level detection or mitigation. The abstract mentions models such as GPT-5.4 and DeepSeek-V3.2; without independent confirmation from the supplied material, those names should be treated as part of the paper’s evaluation description.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Focus on the benchmark framework, the definitions of the fifteen manipulation strategies, and the scenario construction rationale in the appendix. In the experiments, prioritize the model-level risk breakdown, scenario-by-strategy analysis, co-occurrence patterns, and temporal dynamics. For researchers interested in training signals, the most relevant labels are sycophancy, dependency induction, false authority, fact denial, and emotional pressure, since these can expose side effects of instruction tuning and preference optimization.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"安全评测值得扫，重点看场景构造和 prompt perturbation；模型名和结论需谨慎，当作 post-train 风险信号。","en":"Worth skimming for the scenario design and prompt-perturbation analysis; treat the model-specific claims cautiously and use it as a post-training risk signal."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zeyang Yue","Chenfei Yan","Feifei Zhao","Haibo Tong","Mengwen Xu","Xiaozhen Wang","Erliang Lin","Yi Zeng"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["safety-evaluation","multi-turn-dialogue","manipulation","benchmark"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06099","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.06099","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.05548","title":"ADK Arena: Evaluating Agent Development Kits via LLM-as-a-Developer","abstract":"The rapid proliferation of Agent Development Kits (ADKs), SDK-level frameworks for building LLM-powered autonomous agents, has outpaced any empirical understanding of how framework choice affects agent performance. We propose \\textbf{LLM-as-a-Developer}, a methodology that replaces human developers with an LLM coding agent that learns each framework's API from documentation, writes agent code, and iteratively repairs it through a validate-and-feedback loop until tests pass. By holding the develo","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-04T01:00:54Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05548.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“哪个 agent 框架在某个 demo 上更好”，而是一个更基础、也更难做干净的问题：在 ADK（Agent Development Kit）数量快速增长的情况下，如何用可扩展、低人工偏置的方法，系统比较不同框架的两类能力——一类是开发可用性，另一类是最终 agent 效果。传统做法依赖人工为每个框架手写 benchmark 适配代码，成本是 O(N×M)，而且强烈依赖评测者对各框架 API 的熟悉程度，结论很容易被实现质量和个人偏好污染。作者提出的核心设定是把“开发者”本身也标准化：固定同一个 LLM coding agent 去阅读文档、写代码、跑测试、修 bug，只改变底层框架。这样，生成过程中的 token 消耗、修复轮数、失败率可以近似度量 API 学习和使用成本；最终 agent 在 benchmark 上的通过率、成本、延迟则度量框架承载任务的有效性。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，这篇论文不是方法创新型工作，但它对后训练、agent 系统和评测方法有一个明确启发：当系统性能同时受“模型能力”和“外部软件栈”影响时，单纯比较模型已经不够，需要把开发接口、文档质量、默认抽象和运行时约束一起纳入评测。它的价值主要在评测范式，而不在 agent 算法本身。更具体地说，这篇工作把 LLM-as-a-Judge 的思路往前推了一步，变成 LLM-as-a-Developer：不是让模型打分，而是让模型充当统一开发者。这个设定对未来评估 tool-use 框架、训练库、推理服务 SDK，甚至多模态 API，都有可迁移性。对预训练研究员的间接启发在于：模型能力的外显效果，常常被接口摩擦和生态设计放大或压制；如果只看最终任务分数，容易误判模型本身的上限。","technical_approach":"方法上，论文的关键不是某个新 agent 架构，而是把“自动实现 benchmark 适配器”做成了一个可批量运行的评测流水线。整体流程分三层。第一层是框架收集与环境隔离：作者筛出 51 个 Python ADK 框架，为每个框架建立独立 Docker 环境，避免依赖冲突和跨框架污染。第二层是 LLM-as-a-Developer 生成循环：给定一个 benchmark 任务和目标框架，开发者模型可以访问该框架的文档、源码或两者之一，先理解 API，再生成 agent 代码，然后进入 validate-and-feedback 的修复闭环。第三层是三级验证管线：先检查 import 和基础语法，再检查框架级运行正确性，最后在真实 benchmark 任务上执行，捕获运行时错误和行为失败。这个设计的好处是把“代码能不能跑”与“代码跑得好不好”拆开，减少一次性失败导致的评测噪声。\n\n论文还有两个值得注意的设计。其一，作者把生成过程本身当成一等公民来记录，收集 token-level telemetry，包括生成 token、轮数、修复次数、失败类型和美元成本。这样评测输出不只是一个任务分数，而是“达到这个分数花了多少开发代价”。其二，作者专门做了信息源消融：分别给开发者模型提供文档、源码、两者都给、或者都不给，用来区分框架显式文档质量与模型参数记忆的作用。这个问题很关键，因为很多热门框架已经被 frontier coding model 记住了，如果不做消融，很难判断模型到底是在“学文档”还是在“背 API”。\n\n从方法边界看，这套框架并不试图消除所有混杂因素。开发者模型本身的代码能力、上下文窗口、检索策略、修复策略，都会影响结果；benchmark 任务也会偏向某些框架抽象，比如工作流图、tool schema、multi-agent orchestration 或 terminal control。作者的处理方式不是声称绝对公平，而是尽量固定开发者和执行协议，让“框架差异”成为主要变化源。这是合理的工程评测思路。","experiments":"实验覆盖 51 个框架、4 个 benchmark，共 204 个 framework-benchmark 组合，规模上已经明显超过以往手工实现的小样本比较。四个 benchmark 分别覆盖软件工程、对话式工具调用、多工具编排和终端交互，能看到不同框架在不同 agent 形态下的表现差异。\n\n最直接的结果有三组。第一组是开发成本差异。不同框架达到可运行 agent 的生成成本相差 5.6 倍，大致从 0.6 美元到 3.4 美元每个 agent。作者把这解释为 API 复杂度、文档可学性和默认抽象质量的综合体现。像 LangGraph、OpenAI Agents 这类接口相对规整的框架，通常更便宜；体量大、抽象层次混杂或文档不够聚焦的框架，开发成本显著更高。第二组是最终任务效果。最好的单 benchmark ADK agent 任务解决率可到约 80%，而中位框架只有 32% 左右，说明生态里框架质量分布很散，没有单一统治者。第三组是“写 agent 的模型”比“跑 agent 的模型”更重要。论文指出，由 Claude Opus 类开发者模型写出的 agent，即便部署在相同 backbone 上，解决率也大约是 GPT 类开发者模型写出的两倍。这一点很有意思：在 agent 系统里，编程阶段的模型能力会通过代码结构、错误恢复和 API 使用方式长期影响最终表现。\n\n信息源消融也有实质内容。作者发现文档、源码和参数记忆并不是严格替代关系，但三者差距没有想象中大：真实框架使用成功率大致落在 28% 到 40% 区间，源码访问最高；即便不给任何参考材料，仍有约 33% 的 agent 能通过。这说明两件事：一是很多框架 API 已经被大模型部分内化；二是源码常常比文档更直接，因为它暴露了真实调用路径、类型签名和默认行为。对做工具学习或 API grounding 的人，这个结果比单纯 leaderboard 更有信息量。\n\n如果从局限看，论文摘要和正文片段里没有给出所有 benchmark 上逐项最优框架的稳定性分析，也没有充分展开不同开发者模型之间的方差、重试次数敏感性和温度设置影响。这些会影响结果置信度。另一个局限是 benchmark 仍然偏向代码和工具使用场景，不足以代表更开放的长期 agent 任务。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction 和 evaluation problem: how to compare agent development kits at ecosystem scale without relying on hand-written implementations for every framework-benchmark pair. The issue is not just agent quality. It is the combination of API usability, documentation learnability, framework abstractions, and downstream task performance. Manual evaluation is expensive, biased by evaluator familiarity, and hard to scale as the ADK landscape changes quickly. The proposed solution is to standardize the developer itself: keep the same LLM coding agent fixed, let it read docs or source, write the benchmark agent, run tests, and iteratively repair failures. Then vary only the framework. Under this setup, generation effort becomes a proxy for developer-facing usability, while benchmark execution measures framework effectiveness.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is a useful evaluation paper for anyone working on agents, tool use, or model-system interfaces. Its main contribution is methodological. It extends the logic of LLM-as-a-Judge into LLM-as-a-Developer: instead of asking a model to score outputs, it asks a model to act as a controlled developer. That matters because many practical conclusions about agent quality are confounded by framework ergonomics rather than model capability alone. For foundation-model researchers, the indirect lesson is simple: the observable performance of a model can be amplified or suppressed by the software layer wrapped around it. If evaluation ignores interface friction, documentation quality, and runtime defaults, it can misattribute gains or failures to the model itself.","technical_approach":"$e","experiments":"$f","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with the introduction and methodology to understand that the evaluated object is the framework, not the model. Still, the paper does not turn these metrics into a new training signal or provide strong empirical findings about model learning.","technical_approach":"The framework represents each agent run as a normalized event sequence and computes several entropy-based metrics on top of that representation. Action entropy measures how concentrated or diverse the action distribution is. Trajectory entropy measures diversity over full action paths across repeated runs. Tool entropy measures whether tool usage is specialized or scattered. Information gain measures whether uncertainty decreases after evidence gathering, when belief distributions are available. Exploration efficiency combines success with entropy to penalize inefficient randomness. Robustness entropy compares variation in trajectories with variation in outcomes across repeated trials. The implementation is a framework-neutral Python package that converts traces from systems such as LangChain or Google ADK-style logs into a common AgentRun schema, then computes metrics and an optional composite score called EAS. The novelty is not a new benchmark, but an information-theoretic layer for behavioral evaluation.","experiments":"The paper is much closer to a framework proposal and software release than to a heavy empirical study. The provided text does not show large-scale benchmark comparisons, significance testing, or systematic head-to-head evaluation against established agent-assessment methods. The repository, CLI, trace adapters, and plotting utilities are part of the deliverable. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-04T05:18:13Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05718.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"ViCuR Existing methods often condition the teacher on reference answers, rationales, or feedback. Such answer-side privilege is unavailable at inference time, so the student can learn to imitate answer-aware shortcuts instead of grounding its reasoning in the visual input.","importance":"The paper is relevant to the interface between post-training signals and multimodal pretraining objectives. Its main lesson is that a stronger teacher should not rely on information the student can never recover at test time. Visual evidence is a better privileged signal than answer-side information when the goal is grounded reasoning. This also informs multimodal data design: training examples should expose recoverable and verifiable evidence, not only final answers or polished rationales.","technical_approach":"ViCuR replaces answer-based privilege with visual cues: question-relevant evidence derived from the same image or video available to the student at inference. The cue text is only given to the teacher during training, but its source remains present in the standard visual-question input. This reduces the train-test mismatch induced by inaccessible privileged variables. Since the student does not receive cues explicitly, the method adds a lightweight cue recovery module. A designated sink token with dedicated cross-attention parameters is inserted at selected transformer layers. During prefill, this token aggregates task-relevant information from visual tokens into an internal cue-level representation. The module is trained end-to-end only through the distillation objective, keeps the inference interface unchanged, and adds no autoregressive decoding overhead. The same idea applies to privileged-teacher self-distillation and to stronger-teacher OPD.","experiments":"The experiments use Qwen3-VL-2B and Qwen3-VL-8B students across seven multimodal reasoning benchmarks. In stronger-teacher OPD, it improves over OPD baselines by +0.64 and +1.08, and the 8B setting shows consistent out-of-domain gains. These results support the central claim that recoverable visual privilege is more useful for student learning than answer-side privilege.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is appropriate. TTS。","experiments":"模型规模为 2B 参数，训练数据为 1.5M 小时多语种语音。论文报告 dots.tts 在 Seed-TTS-Eval 上取得较好的稳定性和零样本 voice cloning 质量，在 MiniMax 24 语种多语测试集上平均 speaker similarity 领先，并在 EmergentTTS-Eval 与 CV3-Eval reasoning 到最终答案生成的过渡。相比逐步模仿 CoT，LoRi 的主要取舍是放弃 token-level 对应，转而保留 trajectory 的主方向，因此可以把长 CoT 压缩到较短的 latent steps，并且训练上可通过预计算教师低秩表示降低开销。","experiments":"实验覆盖 LLaMA 和 Qwen 等模型族、不同模型规模，以及数学推理任务。论文报告 LoRi 相比已有 iCoT 方法有稳定提升，整体最高约提升 12%，在 GSM8K-Hard 上最高约提升 10%。更大规模模型上，LoRi 能缩小隐式 CoT 与显式 CoT 的差距。关键实验证据包括：教师推理 hidden states 的累计奇异值随 rank 增长很快，支持低秩假设；低秩轨迹对齐比局部状态监督更适合处理长 rationale 到短 latent trajectory well-defined step-by-step correspondence to a student model’s latent reasoning states. Local token-level or sampled-state supervision can miss the global structure of the teacher’s reasoning trajectory.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense. Interventio","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-04T16:20:17Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_06350.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是规则驱动评分场景里的信用分配问题：模型不仅要给出分数，还要让每一步判断都忠于 rubric，并且能被学生答案中的证据支撑。作者认为，面向数学推理设计的 outcome-only RL 或 token 级 credit assignment 不适合这种任务，因为评分错误往往不是单个 token 的局部失误，而是某一步规则调用、证据引用或分档判断偏了，随后整条推理链都会被带偏。于是核心问题变成：如何利用模型内部信号定位“哪一步开始偏离正确分数且失去外部 verifier-guided reasoning、policy-constrained generation、以及需要外部文档 grounding 的任务都有参考价值。贡献量级上，它更像一篇针对特定高结构化任务的训练方法论文，泛化到通用 LLM 训练还需要更多证据，但其中“用内部状态做诊断，再做局部干预”的思路是可以迁移的。","technical_approach":"方法分两阶段。第一阶段是 Edit-SFT。作者先从已有 rollout 中离线提取两类 step-level 内部信号。其一是 posterior belief over final mark：在每个推理步边界，把当前部分推理链接上最终打分 scaffold，读取模型对合法分数 token 的 next-token logits，得到对最终整数分数的后验分布，再计算期望分数和相对 gold mark 的误差变化。这样可以判断某一步是把模型信念推向正确分数，还是把它推远。其二是 grounding signal：通过 mask-based 方式估计当前步骤对学生答案、rubric、以及前序推理的依赖程度，用来判断这一步是否真正建立在外部证据和规则上，而不是在空转。\n\n基于这两个信号，Edit-SFT 不去重写整条 CoT，而是定位“第一个让最终分数后验明显偏离、或 grounding 变弱的原子步骤”，然后只对这一小段做 local atomic edit。修订时提供 rubric checklist 作为 privileged context，帮助模型按评分标准修正，但不强制输出 rigid checklist 格式。这一点很关键：作者想保留自然推理表述，同时提高规则忠实度。\n\n第二阶段是 Edit-RL。这里不是简单做 outcome RL，而是在 group-relative outcome reward 上加入 belief-guided reward shaping。除了最终分数距离 gold mark 的奖励外，还对中途过大的 belief drift 加惩罚。小幅波动被视为正常探索，不罚；只有当某一步让模型对最终分数的信念大幅偏离 gold mark，且超出阈值时才按 excess 程度惩罚。这样做的目的，是把“评分推理应逐步收敛”这个任务结构写进奖励函数，而不是只看最后答对没有。","experiments":"实验在两个真实世界、多学科学生作答评分 benchmark 上进行，并同时看 in-domain 和 out-of-domain 划分。论文主结论是：Edit 相比强 SFT 和 RL baseline 都有稳定优势，而且 OOD 上的收益仍然存在，说明它学到的不只是数据集模板。更重要的是消融。作者明确指出，性能提升的主要来源不是 rubric checklist 这种显式提示，而是 internal-state diagnostics，也就是 posterior belief 和 grounding signal 的定位能力。换句话说，关键不是“给模型更多规则文本”，而是“先找准哪一步错了，再局部修”。摘要片段没有给出完整绝对分数和提升幅度，因此这里不虚构具体数字；但从全文叙述看，最值得关注的是 ablation 是否分别移除 posterior、grounding、belief-shaped RL step。这里是全文最有方法含量的地方。接着看 Edit-RL 的 reward shaping 公式，判断它到底是在做真正的 trajectory calibration，还是只是换一种 token 奖励写法。最后看 ablation 表，尤其是 internal diagnostics、rubric checklist、belief drift penalty 三者分别拿掉后的变化。\n\n预计 20-30 分钟可以完成有效泛读。如果要深挖，建议追问两个问题：一是这种 posterior-over-final-answer 的诊断方式能否迁移到更开放的生成任务；二是 grounding signal 是否足够稳健，还是只在评分这种输入结构清晰的任务里有效。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a credit-assignment problem in rubric-based grading. The model must not only predict the correct score, but also ensure that each intermediate judgment is faithful to the marking scheme and grounded in evidence from the student answer. The authors argue that methods developed for math-style reasoning, such as outcome-only RL or token-level credit reallocation, are a poor fit here. In grading, errors often arise when a reasoning step misapplies a rule, cites weak evidence, or chooses the wrong mark band, and that local mistake then distorts the rest of the chain. The core problem is therefore how to detect the first step where reasoning becomes both score-misaligned and weakly grounded, and then train the model to repair and calibrate that process.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface and to training-signal design for externally constrained reasoning. The most useful idea is that the training signal should not stop at the final outcome. The paper explicitly models the belief trajectory over the final mark during reasoning, and uses that trajectory both for diagnosis and for RL shaping. That is potentially useful beyond grading, especially for verifier-guided reasoning, policy-constrained generation, and tasks that require grounding in external documents or rules. The contribution is best viewed as a task-specific but methodologically interesting post-training paper. Its broader value depends on whether the internal-state diagnostics transfer beyond structured grading settings.","technical_approach":"$12","experiments":"Experiments are run on two real-world, multi-subject student-response grading benchmarks, with both in-domain and out-of-domain splits. The ablations are more informative than the headline numbers. The paper states that the main source of improvement is the internal-state diagnostics, not merely the rubric checklist or extra prompting. In other words, the key gain comes from locating the problematic step using posterior-belief and grounding signals, then editing locally. The provided text does not include the exact absolute scores or deltas, so the most important thing to verify in the full paper is whether removing posterior belief, grounding diagnostics, or belief-shaped RL each causes a meaningful drop.","reading_guide":"A good skim starts with the introduction and the first method figures to fix the task framing: this is not generic reasoning, but externally grounded rule application. w","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-03T16:19:36Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05263.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要解决长程语言 Terminal verifiable rewards can tell whether the final answer or environment state is correct, but they do not tell whether an intermediate reasoning step, retrieval, verification call, or tool action actually contributed to that success.","importance":"This is a post-training RL paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model training because it focuses on the quality of the training signal. Long-horizon agents can satisfy terminal checks while using unsupported evidence chains, drifting beliefs, skipped verification, or evaluator-facing shortcuts. The paper’s main contribution is to make intermediate credit more intervention-aware: a step is useful if replacing it under a specified counterfactual intervention changes final verified success under a frozen continuation policy. This is not an exact causal policy-gradient effect, but it is a more disciplined signal than rewarding steps because they resemble retrieval, reflection, or verification.","technical_approach":"$13","experiments":"The visible methodological details include default cost weights of 1.0, 0.45, 0.18, 0.25, 0.60, and 0.80 for the six constraint categories; intervention aggregation weights of 0.25, 0.20, 0.30, and 0.25; validity thresholds of 0.35 for support and 0.55 for entailment; and a maximum of eight candidate steps per trajectory. The paper reports comparisons against compute-matched and information-matched counterfactual baselines, detector-held-out and human-audited hacking checks, adaptive detector evasion tests, per-seed benchmark uncertainty, refresh-period sensitivity, and model-scale transfer. Exact performance numbers are not available in the supplied text, so precise gains should not be inferred. NLI、情感识别这类主观性较强的任务里，把多标注者分布压成 majority vote，会丢掉不确定性结构，也会让模型学成“过度自信的单点分类器”。作者要解决的是：如何在 LLM 对齐训练中，直接利用 annotator disagreement，而不是只在推理时做 uncertainty elicitation alignment；但它触及一个很重要的训练信号问题：当监督本身是分布而不是点标签时，优化目标应该如何改写。这个问题不只存在于主观任务，也存在于偏好学习、奖励建模、合成数据筛选和多答案推理监督里。论文的启发在于，它没有把歧义当成要清洗掉的脏数据，而是把高分歧样本视为更有信息密度的训练样本，并通过 reward reweighting 让模型显式学习“人类判断分布”。如果把这个思路往更通用的 LLM 训练接口上推，可以联想到 distributional preference learning、uncertainty-aware SFT/RL，以及对 reasoning 任务中多条可接受解路径的建模。","technical_approach":"$14","experiments":"实验覆盖 ChaosNLI、GoEmotions 和 MSP-Podcast，分别对应歧义敏感的自然语言推断和情感识别任务。作者同时看了分布对齐指标和常规分类指标，这一点是合理的，因为只看 F1 会掩盖模型是否真的学到了不确定性结构。\n\n结果上，ChaosNLI 提升最明显：Jensen-Shannon Distance 下降 62.1%，F1 提升 16.6%。这说明模型不仅更接近人类标注分布，而且在离散分类上也没有因为“保留歧义”而变差，反而更好。MSP-Podcast 上 JSD 下降 6.2%，F1 提升 29.2%，分类收益更大，分布收益相对温和，可能说明语音情感任务里类别边界更复杂，保留分歧有助于避免错误塌缩到单标签。摘要没有给出 GoEmotions 的具体数字，但正文表述是持续优于 majority-label supervision。\n\n作者还做了几类分析。第一，高歧义条件下收益更强，符合方法设计预期：如果样本本来就接近单标签，SHALA 的优势不会特别大。第二，对“天然模糊”的语义类别收益更明显，说明模型确实在学习多解释空间，而不是简单做温度变软。第三，模型会表现出更符合不确定性的 reasoning 行为，也就是在输出中更愿意承认多种可能解释。这一点如果有案例分析，会比单纯指标更能说明问题。\n\n从实验解读上，最值得留意的是：JSD 改善是否来自更好的分布建模，还是来自更保守、更平均化的输出。论文声称分类指标也同步提升，这在一定程度上排除了“只会报均匀分布”的坏解，但仍建议读者细看消融，确认动态加权项、reward 设计和 verbalized distribution 解析各自贡献了多少。另一个需要关注的是 rollout 数量、group size 和 reward 方差对训练稳定性的影响，这些通常是 GRPO 类方法的关键超参。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看问题定义和 Figure 1，确认作者到底在优化什么：不是单标签准确率，而是 annotator distribution 对齐。然后看方法部分里 empirical label distribution、verbalized distribution prediction 和基于 GRPO 的 reward 设计，尤其是“按歧义程度动态重加权”这一段，这是方法真正的新意。实验部分优先看主表里的 JSD/F1 结果，再看高歧义子集、类别级分析和案例分析，用来判断模型是否真的学会表达不确定性。若只想抓住主要思想，20 到 30 分钟足够；如果想把它迁移到 preference learning 或 reward modeling，建议再细看附录里的目标函数细节和训练超参。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a training-signal problem that is usually collapsed away in LLM alignment: many human-centered tasks do not have a single correct label. In tasks such as NLI and emotion recognition, annotator disagreement is often part of the phenomenon rather than annotation noise. Standard pipelines reduce multiple annotations to a majority label, which discards uncertainty structure and trains the model to behave like an overconfident point classifier. The paper asks how to align an LLM directly to annotator disagreement during training, instead of only eliciting uncertainty at inference time or applying post-hoc calibration.","importance":"Its direct relevance is stronger for post-training than for pretraining, but the underlying lesson is broader: when supervision is inherently distributional rather than point-valued, the objective should reflect that. This matters not only for subjective classification tasks, but also for preference learning, reward modeling, synthetic-data filtering, and reasoning tasks with multiple acceptable solution paths. The useful idea here is to treat ambiguous examples as informative rather than dirty. SHALA-LLM gives higher training influence to samples with richer disagreement structure and explicitly optimizes toward human judgment distributions. That makes it relevant to uncertainty-aware alignment and distributional supervision more generally.","technical_approach":"$15","experiments":"$16","reading_guide":"Start with the problem setup and Figure 1 to understand that the target is annotator-distribution alignment rather than single-label accuracy. Then read the method section carefully around three pieces: empirical label distributions, verbalized distribution prediction, and the GRPO reward design. The key novelty is the ambiguity-aware dynamic reweighting, so that part deserves the most attention. In the experiments, first inspect the main JSD and F1 tables, then move to the high-ambiguity subsets, category-level analyses, and qualitative examples to see whether the model truly learns to represent uncertainty. A 20 to 30 minute skim is enough to capture the main idea; if you want to adapt it to preference learning or reward modeling, spend more time on the appendix for objective details and training hyperparameters.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不改 pretrain，但会改你看待 preference signal 的方式：别再默认单标签干净，先看它如何把 ambiguity 变成训练信号；只读方法和消融即可。","en":"It won’t change pretraining directly, but it may change how you treat preference signals: stop assuming collapsed labels are clean; read the method and ablations only."},"metadata":{"institutions":["MIT"],"authors":["Jingyao Wu","Ashley Wang","Keane Ong","Paul Pu Liang","Rosalind Picard"],"notable_authors":["Rosalind Picard","Paul Pu Liang"],"tags":["alignment","rlhf","annotator-disagreement","ambiguity-modeling","human-feedback"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05376","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.05376","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.05516","title":"Dominant-Layer ZO: A Single Layer Dominates Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of LLMs","abstract":"Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables memory-efficient fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward passes, but it remains unclear how useful adaptation is distributed across layers. In this work, we reveal a surprising phenomenon: ZO fine-tuning is sharply dominated by a single decoding layer. Across multiple LLM families and downstream tasks, fine-tuning this dominant layer alone consistently matches or even exceeds full-model ZO fine-tuning. We further show that the dominan","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-03T23:42:09Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05516.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要回答的问题很具体：在只用 forward、靠随机扰动估计梯度的 zeroth-order (ZO) 微调里，模型的“有效可学习性”到底分布在哪些层？作者发现它并不均匀，而是几乎被某一个解码层（dominant layer）垄断；进一步的问题是：能否在训练前、无需逐层试错，就把这层找出来，并解释为什么会这样。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在“又一个微调技巧”，而在于它揭示了一个与预训练结构性质强相关的训练动态：ZO 的学习信号在残差网络里会被某些早期层放大并主导更新，而这种主导层与 activation outlier 的出现位置稳定绑定、且对任务不敏感。这给两类工作提供了抓手：\n1) 训练/微调接口：当只能做 forward-only 更新（低显存、隐私场景、黑盒 API、或大规模在线持续学习）时，应该把预算集中在少数结构位置，而不是“全模型均匀扰动”。\n2) 预训练机制侧：activation outlier 层不仅影响量化/稳定性，也可能决定某些优化范式下的可塑性（plasticity）瓶颈位置，提示在预训练阶段对 outlier 的形成（归一化、激活分布、残差尺度）做控制，可能改变后续可适配性。","technical_approach":"方法分三步：\n1) 逐层隔离实验（discovery）：基于 MeZO/SPSA 两点估计，固定同一套 ZO 超参（扰动尺度、学习率、batch 等），每次只更新某一层参数，其余层冻结，得到“单层 ZO 微调曲线”。作者观察到：绝大多数层几乎不带来收益，只有一个层在多个任务上稳定达到/超过全模型 ZO 微调。\n2) dominant layer 的性质刻画：它对任务基本不变（task-agnostic），但对模型家族/具体模型是固定的（model-specific）。同时对照 FO（反向传播）微调：FO 的收益更分散，不出现单层长期统治，说明这是 ZO 特有的层间信号分配现象，而不是“这层本来就最重要”。\n3) 训练前识别（identification）：作者把 dominant layer 与 activation outlier 现象联系起来。做法是用一个小的校准集只跑 inference，统计各层激活的异常大值模式（outlier pattern），选择“第一个出现明显 outlier 的层”。经验上该层与 dominant layer 对齐，从而避免逐层 ZO 试跑。\n机制解释上，作者给出一个与 ZO 估计方差/信噪比相关的直觉：ZO 的更新方向来自最终 loss 对随机参数扰动的响应幅度。越能在 forward 里引起更大、更稳定的 loss 变化的层，ZO 的梯度估计越可靠。早期层的扰动会进入 residual stream 并传播到后续所有层，经过多次变换累积，最终对 loss 的影响更大；而“第一处 outlier 层”往往意味着该层在残差通道里已经形成高幅值/高敏感度的方向，使得扰动更容易被放大并在末端体现为可测的 loss 差分。","experiments":"论文在两种模型上做了系统验证：LLaMA2-7B 与 Qwen3-8B；任务覆盖 9 个下游集合（包含分类/多选/生成，如 WSC、COPA、DROP 等）。关键结论与数字：\n- 单层 dominant-layer ZO 微调：在多任务上达到与“全模型 MeZO”相当，且经常更好。\n- 平均分数：相对全模型 MeZO 平均提升约 0.86 个百分点；相对 LoRA-based 的 ZO 微调平均提升约 0.61 个百分点（作者把 LoRA 也放在 ZO 框架下做对比）。\n- 训练效率：相对全模型 MeZO，速度提升约 1.12× 到 4.52×（取决于任务/配置）。\n- 现象稳定性：dominant layer 对任务稳定，但在不同模型上层号不同；并且与“第一处 activation outlier 层”高度一致。\n（从全文叙述看，最关键的消融不是换任务，而是：逐层单独更新 vs 全模型更新；ZO vs FO 的层间对比；以及 inference-only 的 outlier 统计能否在训练前正确命中 dominant layer。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 20–30 分钟）：\n- 先看 layer-wise 单层微调的主结果图（通常是 Figure 1 一类的逐层曲线/柱状图）：这是全文的“现象证据”。重点看“多数层无效、单层接近全模型”的幅度差异，而不是绝对分数。\n- 再看 dominant layer 的 task-agnostic / model-specific 证据：同一模型跨任务是否命中同一层，不同模型是否换层。\n- 然后看 inference-only 识别方法：outlier 统计具体用什么激活（残差流/MLP 输出/attention 输出）、用什么指标判定“outlier 出现”，以及校准集大小对命中率的影响。\n- 最后看机制解释部分：抓住“ZO 依赖 loss 差分信号强度/稳定性”与“早期残差传播导致扰动效应累积”这条因果链。读的时候可以带着一个问题：如果把残差尺度、RMSNorm/LayerNorm、或 outlier 抑制（clipping/normalization）改掉，dominant layer 会不会移动或消失？作者是否做了相关验证。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a very concrete question about forward-only (zeroth-order, ZO) fine-tuning of LLMs: when gradients are estimated from loss differences under random perturbations, where does useful adaptation actually happen across layers? The authors report a strong concentration effect—one single decoder layer dominates ZO fine-tuning. They further ask whether this dominant layer can be identified before training (without layer-wise trial runs) and why such dominance emerges specifically under ZO rather than first-order (FO) backprop.","importance":"The main value for pretraining-oriented readers is the training-dynamics insight tied to pretrained model internals: ZO learning signal is not evenly distributed in deep residual Transformers; it is amplified and becomes learnable mainly at a specific early layer that coincides with the first activation-outlier layer. This links three topics that are usually discussed separately—activation outliers (often in quantization), residual-stream propagation, and optimization signal-to-noise in ZO estimators. Practically, it suggests that in forward-only adaptation regimes (memory-constrained tuning, black-box APIs, or certain continual adaptation settings), spending the perturbation/update budget on a carefully chosen layer can match full-model ZO while being cheaper. Conceptually, it hints that pretraining choices that shape outlier formation and residual scaling may also shape downstream plasticity under non-standard optimization.","technical_approach":"$17","experiments":"They evaluate on LLaMA2-7B and Qwen3-8B across 9 downstream tasks (including WSC, COPA, DROP; spanning classification/multiple-choice/generation). Key reported numbers:\n- Dominant-layer-only ZO fine-tuning matches or exceeds full-model MeZO across tasks.\n- Average score improves by ~0.86 percentage points over full-model MeZO, and by ~0.61 points over a LoRA-based ZO baseline.\n- Training speedup vs full-model MeZO ranges from ~1.12× to ~4.52×.\nThe most informative ablations are: (i) layer-wise single-layer tuning curves vs full-model ZO, (ii) ZO vs FO layer-wise gain distribution, and (iii) whether the inference-only outlier-based selector correctly predicts the dominant layer before any tuning.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (20–30 minutes):\n- Start with the main layer-wise plots (the figure showing performance when tuning each layer alone). Focus on the gap between the dominant layer and the rest, not the absolute task scores.\n- Check the task-agnostic/model-specific evidence: same layer across tasks for one model, different layer across model families.\n- Read the inference-only dominant-layer identification procedure carefully: which activations are measured, what statistic defines an outlier, and how sensitive it is to calibration set size.\n- Finally, read the mechanism section with one concrete question in mind: if residual scaling / normalization / outlier suppression changes, would the dominant layer shift or disappear, and do they test that?","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 dominant layer 与 activation outlier 的对应关系；若结果稳，应进低显存微调 ablation。","en":"Read the dominant-layer/activation-outlier link; if the effect holds, it belongs in memory-constrained tuning ablations."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Wanhao Yu","Ziyan Wang","Zheng Wang","Abeer Matar Almalky","Yihang Zuo","Shuteng Niu","Sen Lin","Adnan Siraj Rakin","Deliang Fan","Li Yang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["zeroth-order-optimization","LLM-finetuning","activation-outliers","parameter-efficient-tuning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05516","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2606.05516","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2606.05988","title":"Compress-Distill: Reasoning Trace Compression for Efficient Knowledge Distillation","abstract":"Reasoning models produce long chain-of-thought traces that are costly to distill and encourage verbose student outputs. We study post-hoc compression of such traces before knowledge distillation. Two teachers, Qwen3.5-397B-A17B and gpt-oss-120B, generate about 283k correct traces each; two instruction-tuned models then compress them to 8.6-21.0% of their original character length. Across a 48-run main grid plus seven Qwen-teacher truncation ablations, compressed traces reduce training tokens to ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-04T10:30:58Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2606_05988.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的是一个很实际的蒸馏问题：大推理模型生成的 chain-of-thought 太长，拿它做监督既贵，又会把学生模型也教得很啰嗦。作者关心的是，能不能先把 teacher trace 压缩，再拿压缩后的 trace 做蒸馏，从而在训练成本、推理长度和最终准确率之间找到一个更好的折中。全文的答案比较克制：可以明显省算力和 token，但不是免费午餐；在他们测试的范围内，raw trace 始终给出最高准确率，compression 更像一种工程上的 cost-quality trade-off，而不是更优监督信号。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接相关性有限，因为它是典型 post-train / distillation 研究，不是基座预训练 recipe。但它对“推理数据该怎么喂给学生”这个接口问题有现实启发。第一，它量化了长 CoT 监督的边际收益和代价，说明 reasoning trace 的信息密度并不容易通过简单重写保留下来。第二，它提醒人们不要把“更短、更干净的 trace”默认当成更好的蒸馏目标；压缩过程本身会丢掉对小模型有帮助的冗余、局部校验和解题节奏。第三，它给出一个很有用的对照：长度匹配的 raw truncation。这个对照让人能区分“压缩 trace 有帮助”到底是因为 supervision 更优，还是只是因为 token 更少。结果偏向后者：压缩主要带来的是成本收益，而不是更高的信息效率。","technical_approach":"方法分三段。第一段是 teacher trace 生成。作者用两个大教师模型 Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 和 gpt-oss-120B，对训练题目做带 reasoning 的采样，抽取 <think> 中的 trace 和最终答案；如果答案不对，就 rejection sampling 重试，直到得到正确样本或达到上限。这样构造出的训练集只保留“题目 + 正确答案 + 正确推理轨迹”。\n\n第二段是 trace compression。作者不用专门训练压缩器，而是直接拿两个 instruction-tuned 模型做后处理压缩器，用统一 prompt 要求“保留关键逻辑步骤、关键洞察和最终答案，但尽量更短”。两个压缩器压缩风格不同，压缩强度也不同，形成两套并行数据。这里的设计重点是：压缩发生在蒸馏前，属于 post-hoc rewriting，而不是让 teacher 在生成时天然更短。\n\n第三段是 student fine-tuning。学生模型在三种 supervision 上训练：raw trace、compressed trace、answer-only。训练目标仍是标准 next-token prediction，只在 assistant token 上算 loss，并做 sample packing。作者同时比较 LoRA 和 full/FSDP 一轮训练，覆盖四个学生模型：Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base、Qwen3.5-9B-Base、Llama-3.1-8B、gpt-oss-20B。\n\n全文最关键的方法设计其实不是压缩本身，而是 ablation。作者额外做了一个 length-matched raw-trace truncation：把原始 Qwen trace 按最激进压缩版本的 token 长度截断，保持最终答案不变。这个对照很重要，因为它把训练 token 预算控制住了。如果压缩 trace 真有“更高信息密度”，它应该优于同长度的原始截断 trace；而实验没有支持这种说法。换句话说，压缩器更多是在做成本裁剪，而不是把 teacher reasoning 提炼成更适合学生学习的表示。","experiments":"数据和实验网格都比较完整。教师有两个：Qwen3.5-397B-A17B 与 gpt-oss-120B。作者各自生成约 28.3 万条正确 trace。压缩后，trace 长度降到原字符长度的 8.6% 到 21.0%。训练 token 预算降到 raw 的 12% 到 30%，训练 wall-clock 加速 2.0 到 7.6 倍，学生推理输出长度缩短 3 到 19 倍；其中 gpt-oss teacher 原始 trace 本来更短，所以压缩带来的缩短幅度相对更小。\n\n主实验一共 48 个 run：两位教师、四个学生、四种数据源、两种训练方式。结论很一致：在所有评估尺度和两个教师下，raw traces 的下游准确率始终最高；compressed traces 居中；answer-only 最便宜，但效果最差，而且在 full fine-tuning 下不稳定。这个结论比“压缩有效”更重要，因为它说明长 trace 里确实有被压缩器损失掉的监督价值。\n\n作者还做了 7 个 Qwen-teacher 的 truncation ablation。这个部分是全文最值得看的实验之一。长度匹配后，compressed trace 并没有稳定优于同 token 预算下的 raw truncation，说明压缩的收益主要来自减少 token，而不是创造了更优 supervision。另一个有用观察是小模型会出现 termination failure，answer-only 训练尤其容易不稳定，这提示 reasoning supervision 可能还承担了“输出结构锚定”的作用，而不只是传递解题步骤。\n\n评测覆盖 in-distribution 的数学、科学、常识/逻辑、医学题集，以及若干 OOD reasoning 数据。虽然摘要里没有逐项列出所有准确率数字，但全文的实验组织足够支持其核心结论：压缩 trace 是一个可接受的效率折中，不是性能增益方法。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，重点不是某个新算法，而是实验设计是否把问题问清楚。建议先看 Method，尤其是三阶段 pipeline 和 length-matched truncation ablation。然后直接看主结果表，确认 raw/compressed/answer-only 三者在不同学生和教师上的排序是否稳定。最后看 failure mode 分析，特别是小模型 termination failure 和 answer-only 的不稳定性。\n\n如果只看 20 分钟，优先读：1）数据构造与 rejection sampling；2）压缩比例和训练/推理成本表；3）长度匹配截断对照；4）不同学生规模下的结果差异。真正值得记住的不是“压缩能省多少”，而是“为什么压缩后仍然掉点”：压缩器删掉的往往不是纯废话，而是对学生学习有帮助的冗余中间态、局部验证和格式支架。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical distillation question: reasoning teachers produce very long chain-of-thought traces, which are expensive to train on and also encourage verbose student outputs. The authors ask whether those traces can be compressed before distillation, and whether the resulting supervision gives a better cost-quality trade-off. The answer is careful and useful: compression saves a lot of tokens and time, but it does not beat raw traces on accuracy. It is an efficiency trade-off, not a free improvement.","importance":"This is more of a post-training and distillation paper than a pretraining one, but it matters at the pretrain–posttrain interface. It quantifies how much useful supervision is carried by long reasoning traces, and how much of that signal survives post-hoc rewriting. The main lesson is that shorter traces are not automatically better traces. Compression removes cost, but it also removes redundancy, local checks, and pacing cues that may help smaller students learn. The length-matched truncation control is especially valuable because it separates two hypotheses: compressed traces are better supervision versus compressed traces are simply cheaper supervision. The results support the latter much more strongly.","technical_approach":"$18","experiments":"$19","reading_guide":"This is best read as an empirical trade-off study.
